tiruvallikkēṇi - 600 005.
yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷai nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalaik kaṭaintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmai kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunilai niṉṟu ārāyn tirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmaikaḷai ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vaittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum kaṟkum tollaiyai mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tillai, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēvaiyumillai. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmaikaḷai ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷaip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉai aṭaiyalām.
- maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nākai - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭaittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulamai mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamaiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulam uṭaiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟainta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivait tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittokai, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭaimaiyē eṉṉai ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ tokai curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟaippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷai varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭaiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.

vaṇṇārappēṭṭai	maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntarampiḷḷai
tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

maṟainta aṭikaḷār! tamiḻ ñāyiṟu
āṅkilam, āriyam, tīntamiḻ - eṉṟa mūṉṟaiyum tuṟaipōka muṟṟakkaṟṟa oru mūtaṟiñar entam aṭikaḷ. mummoḻikaḷaiyum orucēra ivvāṟu tamiḻulakil mācaṟak kaṟṟa nallāciriyar ivarkku muṉṉumillai; ivarkkup piṉṉu millai. itu tamiḻ varalāṟu.
vaṭamoḻiyum tamiḻum vēṟu vēṟu eṉpatai viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉār. āciriyar civañāṉamuṉivar. (18ām nūṟ.) vaṭa moḻiyai aṟavē akaṟṟiviṭṭut tamiḻtaṉittu iyaṅkavallatu eṉpatai ārāyntu coṉṉār ṭākṭar kālṭuvel (19ām nūṟ.) avvāṟu pallāyiram ēṭukaḷ tamiḻil eḻutik kāṭṭiṉār āciriyar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ. (20ām nūṟ.) moḻi varalāṟṟil inta mūṉṟum mūṉṟu māṇikka uṇmaikaḷ.
aṭikaḷār ēṟṟiya viḷakku iṉṉum erikiṉṟatu cāṉṟōrē! emmai maṟantālum yām maṟakka māṭṭēmāl, nīvīr aṭivaittu naṭanta avvaḻiyai ematu neñcu nāṭukiṟatu. aṭikaḷ maṟainta atticai nōkki entalai tāḻvatāka!
- vittuvāṉ a. kiruṣṇamūrtti 
               (pak. 48)

maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
nuḻaivurai
 taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
 tavattiru. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
muntai aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum takavārnta perumai peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇaiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yuraikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟai moḻikaḷukkum tantai moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻpparapāḷarāyp potunilaik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum perumai peṟṟa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭai malaiyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpatai nilai nāṭṭiya niṟaimalai, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
  ainteḻuttāl orupāṭai eṉṟu aṟaiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭaiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalaimali pēḻaiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟaimai pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpatai,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
marapunilai tirital ceyyuṭ killai 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇai ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citaintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumaiyum kuṟumaiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷai ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟaimalai aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻai mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takainiṉṟa taṇṭamiḻai - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilaikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟaimalai yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talaippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivai
 paintamiḻ kākkum paṭai
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭaiyum kalait tiṟap paṭaippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭaiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟai āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟai āṟṟalai 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalaic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattai
paṉimalai mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amaiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amaintavar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimalai - imayam; nīla āṟu - nail; amaitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷai, nūlaka iyakkat tantai araṅka nātarum, innāḷ caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakaimaippaṭutti vaittamai perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avai,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟaiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭurai 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nākai aṭikaḷai - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattaic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakaiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammaiyār! ivviṇaiyar koṭaiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉai ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷattai vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟaivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvarai payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum nilaiyil, arumait tantaiyār iyaṟkai eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉaiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyait toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmai tamiḻukku illai. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppillai eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟaikaḷaiyum iḷamaiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷaip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟaiyaiyum, nākaiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟaiyaiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟaiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟaiyāl vāṭum aṉṉaikkuc cumaiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākaiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamaiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉai māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpataiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyai niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, tokai nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvai, iṟaiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvai mukappil, ematu patiṉaintām āṇṭil muṟaippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōvaiyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷaiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷaiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyataiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uraiyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭaiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamaip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nākai intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc caiva cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉai maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉaipeyaril maṟuppurai eḻutiṉār. ataṉaikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl murukavēḷaic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉai viraivilē ceṉṉaikku varuvippōm nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉai vāḻvu amaiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇaivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉai māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇiya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumai kaṇṭu, pērāciriyaraik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uraiyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattaiyum mūtaṟivaiyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanilai ovvāmaiyāl nākaikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉai: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaraic ceṉṉaikku aḻaittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpikai eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉai kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra akkallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟait talaivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kalaiñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkalai māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullaippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappālai (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci urai eḻutiṉār.
avvurai nūlai vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉait tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟaiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvuraip pāṅkiṉaip piṉpaṟṟip paḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷai ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟai urai, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakaikaḷai iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉai viḷaittamai kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakaiya uraikaḷaiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻaintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭa moḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭattai viruppap pāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉaik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikkai kuṟaiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟaiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyai muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikaimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. vaiyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikaimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭaivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉai kacaṭukaḷai nīkkiyatu? vaiyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammaic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalaik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟaic ceyta arumai eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal, kārikai āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārait taḻuvutal uṇmai tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapurattai vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkīkāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattaiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattaiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmikaḷ valiyuṟuttiya avviraṇṭu koḷkaikaḷaiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷai oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. innilaiyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkaikaḷaiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉaikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉaiyār ciṉṉammaiyār, tuṇaiviyār cavuntiram ammai yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvaraik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpikai, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. innilaiyilēyē 27.08.1911il caivat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭai (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. muṉṉēyē avarai aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvai
nām mīḷpārvai oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akavaiyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kaivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullaippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappālai ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvurai vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉai; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉai; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉai - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpikaiyār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭamai eṉpatu, akattē amaintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷai viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyām. av veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravillai! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka nilaiyam, potunilaik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amaippu uruvākum nilaiyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avai iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa nilaiyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭait tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭait tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭait tūṇṭal valimai mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyai veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakaittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takaimai ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉai uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilaināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amaintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmai,
 tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟai yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantaip pēṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉaippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟokai, arumaṇik kuvaiyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷaik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, caivacittānta nūṟ patippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟkoṭaiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumaimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumait tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 varai mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavai. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyavaiyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭaimai ākkap paṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vaitta kuṟuvaṭṭukaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉamai, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷai mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. annilaiyil,
ūḻaiyum uppakkam kāṇpar ulaiviṉṟit
  tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyai vāymai moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷaiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amaintu tāṅkal, iyaṟkai iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟai muṉvaittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappukkām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icaik kalaikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākarattai veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉai vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉaip pakutikaḷaiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, uraivēntar auvai cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷai vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantaiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷaiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷaiyum muḻumai muḻumaiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - tokai - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷaiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti varai veḷivanta - tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭaimai ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷai ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amaikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumai iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇaiyaiyum makkaḷaiyum uṟavaiyum aṉpaiyum naṇpaiyum oruṅkē iṇaittu, aṟiñar perumakkaḷ palar aravaṇaippaiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
maṟaimalaiyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumai! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalaic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭait tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putaiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉaiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭai! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakaimai! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟaiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymai viḷakkamām!
puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar nilaiyam,							      iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,							                irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturai - 6

malaikka vaikkum maṟaimalaiyam!
patippurai
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉai vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmai uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷatai varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇarai aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭaiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattait tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭai eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭai niṉaivu kūrum vakaiyil, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭattai niṟaivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyai eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭaippukaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimaiyūṭṭiṉar.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivukkaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻai 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭaippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavai, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavai, peyar terintum pārvaikkuk kiṭṭātavai - eṉap palavakaiyāyk kiṭaittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭaippukaḷ! avaṟṟai `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum talaippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭaivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavalaiyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt ticai nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭaipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭaiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputaiyal palar kaikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷārait tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyattai etirppatilum camaṟkirutattai vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēmpāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyait tamiḻar neñcaṅkaḷil arumpa vaittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavaittatu taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻaippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimaiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻai mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukaiyaiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kalaiñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitaikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvai avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta virutai civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamaippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malaimalaiyaṭikaḷ).
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyaraik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟaimiku āṅkilam maṟaivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulamai mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻaikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumaiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
  maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷ maṟaiyāt tiruppeyar
  vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉaip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉai maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkaiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻaippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamillai.
ceṉṉaiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷait tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talaimuṟaikkum varuṅkālat talaimuṟaikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉai (cātaṉai)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷaik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumaiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avai kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpatai aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalattai otukki vaittu, tamiḻ nalattaik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉaittu nūlkaḷaiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟaivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalaip piṉṉiṇaippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻaikkum uṇarvai iḷamaiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmai eḻavaittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avarai eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamaiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟaivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavai! avaṟṟil orupakutiyai yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kaikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vai. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!
tamiḻilakkiyap peruñcelvattai mīṇṭum mīṇṭum accākki, aṉaivar kaikaḷaiyum eṭṭac ceyta perumai - caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, u.vē.cā. nūlakam, maṇivācakar patippakam mutaliya patippāḷarkaḷukku uriyatu.
accuttuṟai aṟimukamāṉa piṉpē, aṟivuttuṟai aṉaivarukkum potuccottākum vāyppu vantatu. eṭṭāk kaṉiyāka ōlaic cuvaṭikaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭanta ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷ, ellōrukkum eḷitāyk kiṭaikkum paṇiyaic ceyta patippāciriyarkaḷum, patippāḷarkaḷum nam naṉṟik kuriyōr!
iravu pakal pārātu mutumaiyaik karutātu uḻaikkum maṉavurattai muṉṉōṭip patippāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇiyē emakku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
aracum, palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum, camaya pīṭaṅkaḷum ,perum iyakkaṅkaḷum, perum celvarkaḷum ceyya vēṇṭiya perun tamiḻppaṇi itu! eḷiyavarkaḷāl iyaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peruntamiḻp paṇikaḷai iṉṉalkaḷukku iṭaiyil arumuyaṟciyōṭu ceytu varuvatu, uḷḷavuram oṉṟiṉāltāṉ!
vallamai cērkkum valimaiyuṇ ṭākkum!
  vaṇṭamiḻ naintiṭil etunammaik kākkum?
kākkum tamiḻaik kākkum uṇarvaip pāvēntar pāṭal varikaḷ tantaṉa.
accukku eṭṭāmalum kaikkuk kiṭṭāmalum irunta pēraṟiñar palariṉ nūlkaḷai nāṭṭuṭaimaiyākki tirāviṭar iyakka aracukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, aṟivu paravalākum vāyppukaḷ uruvāyiṉa.
`carivum iḻappum namakku vantālum tamiḻukku varakkūṭātu eṉa eṇṇi uḻaikkum iyakkaṅkaḷum amaippukaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaṟṟāmal vaḷarntapaṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa.
tiruviṭar kaḻakam (1908)
taṉittamiḻ iyakkam (1916)
teṉṉintiya nalaurimaik kaḻakam (1916)
ceṉṉai mākāṇat tamiḻccaṅkam (1934)
tamiḻppātukāppuk kaḻakam (1937)
tamiḻaṟiñar kaḻakam (1943)
tamiḻp pērāyam (1959)
tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakam (1961)
taṉittamiḻk kaḻakam (1964)
ulakattamiḻk kaḻakam (1968)
tamiḻiyakkam (1972)
tamiḻc cāṉṟōr pēravai (1998)
tamiḻ malarccikkum taṉittamiḻ vaḷarccikkum aṭittaḷam amaitta ittaku amaippukaḷukku mūla vicaiyākat tikaḻum perumaiyaip peṟuvōr maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārum pāvāṇarumē!
tamiḻ vaḷattiṟkum tamiḻar nalattiṟkum civaṉiya mēmpāṭṭiṟkum maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ aḷappariyavai. avai toṭarpāka avar āyntaḷitta aṟivu muttukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavai!
aṭikaḷāriṉ aṉaittup paṭaippukaḷum poruḷvaḻip pirikkappaṭṭu, kālavaricaiyil 34 tokuppukaḷākat tokukkap paṭṭu `maṟaimalaiyam eṉa uṅkaḷ kaikaḷil ippōtu tavaḻkiṉṟatu.
pala talaimuṟaikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum aṟivuk karuvūlam itu!
tamiḻar illantōṟum irukkavēṇṭiya aruntamiḻc celvam itu!
aṟivulakattāl `maṟaimalaiyam varavēṟkappaṭum eṉṉum nampikkai emakkuṇṭu. nūṟ curaṅkattil nuḻaiya aḻaikkiṟōm.
 taṉakkeṉa vāḻvatu cāvukkoppākum
  tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻvatē vāḻvatākum
eṉum pāvēntar varikaḷai niṉaivuk kūṟukiṟōm.
- kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
ituvarai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa

patippāḷar vivaram
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ 
piṟanta nāḷ	:	3.7.1948
piṟanta ūr	:	uṟantairāyaṉkuṭikkāṭu añcal						urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625							tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi	:	kallūri pukumuka vakuppu
ippōtaiya toḻil	:	puḻakkattil illāta paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷait 					tēṭiyeṭuttu veḷiyiṭal
āṟṟiyuḷḷa potuppaṇikaḷ
1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉaippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷaippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟaiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantairāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam eṉum camūka amaippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimaik kaḻakam eṉṉum amaippaiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattaiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇaintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻaittavar. iḷam talaimuṟaikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkaiyai neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amaippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭamaṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻaittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakattai niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyum, putiya paṭaippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷaiyum, 19  ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vaittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷai tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumaiyākac cērttu poruḷvaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil orē vīccil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttirai patittu varupavar.
potunilai
taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, tantai periyār, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā, moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar,  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār ākiyōrai vaḻikāṭṭikaḷākak koṇṭu avartam koḷkaikaḷai niṟaivēṟṟa ayarātu uḻaittu varupavar.

maṟaimalaiyam tokuppil iṭampeṟāta nūlkaḷ
mutaṟkuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam (1898), tukaḷaṟu pōtam (1898), cittānta ñāṉapōtam catamaṇik kōvai kuṟippurai (1898), vēta civākamap piramāṇyam (1900),  inta nāṉku nūlkaḷum tamiḻakattiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil tēṭiyum eṅkaḷ kaikkuk kiṭaikka villai. ātalāṉ, maṟaimalaiyam tokuppil mēṟkaṇṭa nūlkaḷ iṭam peṟavillai.

nūlurai
iḷaiñarkkāṉa iṉṟamiḻ

itaṉ mutaṟpatippu aṭikaḷār viruppa āvaṇappaṭi 1957 il veḷivantatu.
paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷil payilum ciṟārkku iṉiya eḷiya tiruttamāṉa taṉittamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭa pāṭanūlkaḷ illāp peruṅkuṟaiyai nīkkum poruṭṭu 1934 il ciṟuvarkkāṉa centamiḻ eṉṉum nūl eḻutiyataic cuṭṭum aṭikaḷ nōkkaik kuṟippiṭṭu, itukāl iḷaiñarkkāṉa iṉṟamiḻ veḷiyiṭuvataip patippurai veḷippaṭuttukiṟatu.
tiṅkaḷait toḻutal mutalāka makkaḷ vāḻkkai īṟāka 13 kaṭṭuraikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu iḷaiñarkkāṉa iṉṟamiḻ.
iṉimai tantu makiḻvippatāl, `tiṅkaḷ eṉṟum,
nāḷum vaḷartalāl, `kalaiyōṉ eṉṟum,
iravil viḷaṅkutalāl `iravōṉ eṉṟum, alavaṉ eṉṟum
`allōṉ eṉṟum
kālavarai yaṟai ceytalāl `mati eṉṟum
iṭai iṭaiyē kaṟaiyuṭaimaiyāl, `kaḷaṅkaṉ eṉṟum piṟai nilaiyil vaḷainta tōṟṟam tarutalāl, `kuraṅki eṉṟum ak kaṟai muyalpōl tōṟṟam tarutalāl, `muyaṟkūṭu eṉṟum peyar peṟum eṉpataip poruḷoṭu puṇarttu viḷakkukiṟār.
tēytal uṇmaiyum perukal uṇmaiyum
  māytal uṇmaiyum piṟattal uṇmaiyum
  aṟiyā tōraiyum aṟiyak kāṭṭum
arumaiyai uṟaiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār pāṭiya puṟappāṭal vaḻiyil kāṭṭukiṟār.
ivvāṟē eṭutta poruḷiṉ peyar viḷakkam - payaṉ viḷakkam - cāṉṟu viḷakkam eṉak kāṭṭik kaṭṭurai eḻutum payiṟciyil vallarām  vakaiyil nūl yāttuḷār.
`nilaiyāṉam eṉpatai, `nerunal uḷaṉoruvaṉ iṉṟillai eṉpataṟku maṭṭum 14 cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭukiṟār.
`kālatiyaṉ kiricelāṉ katai eṉpatoru katai iṭam peṟukiṟatu. ittāliya nāṭṭuk katai. ik katai virivuṭaiyatu. maṇaṅ koḷḷa virumpāta maṉṉaṉai nāṭṭumakkaḷ maṇaṅkoḷḷa vaṟpuṟutta, avaṉ. yāṉ eṉṉuṭaiya kaṇkaḷaik koṇṭē oru maṇamakaḷait tēṭip peṟa vēṇṭumē aṉṟip piṟaruṭaiya kaṇkaḷaik koṇṭu avaḷait tēṭi aṭaital kūṭātu eṉṟu kūṟi avvāṟē tēṭi aṭaintu, mēṉmaiyuṟṟa katai atuvām. paḻantamiḻar kātal vāḻviṉ `piḻicāṟu aṉaiyatu itu.
tiruvaḷḷuva mālai - kapilar akaval mutalām piṟkālap puṉaivil aṭikaḷ nampikkai koṇṭatu, nampamuṭiyātatākavē uḷḷamai uṇmaiyām! tiruvaḷḷuvar, tiruvaḷḷuvar vāḻkkai, aṟañceykai, vaḷḷuvar, vaḷḷuvam paṟṟiyavai.
kāṉat tōkai tēvamaṇi katai eṉpatu, uḷḷotta kātalarākat tikaḻnta kāṉat tōkaiyum tēvamaṇiyum, avarkaḷ peṟṟērkaḷāl veṟṟipeṟa iyalā nilaiyil, iruvarum orutuṟavumaṭam cārntu oruvarai oruvar aṟiyārāy vāḻntu, aṟinta piṉṉarum attuṟavil nilaitta peruvāḻvu uraippatām. nallapaṭaippu. maṉōṉmaṇīyam cuntaraṉār paṭaippāṉa civakāmi caritattiṉ cāyal ik kataiyamaippil pulappaṭukiṉṟatu!
- irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ

accaṭippu maṟṟum nūl kaṭṭamaippu:  
veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭarcu maṟṟum pirācacu intiyā						
`maṟaimalaiyam ellā nilaiyilum ceppamuṟa veḷi varuvataṟkup pallāṟṟāṉum utaviyāka irunta ivarkaḷukkum maṟṟum aṟiñar perumakkaḷukkum em niṟainta  naṉṟiyum pārāṭṭum.

ciṟuvarkkāṉa centamiḻ
1964il pāri nilaiyam mūṉṟām patippāka veḷiyiṭṭa 
nūliṉai mūlamākak koṇṭu innūl patippikkappaṭukiṟatu.
innūlaip paṟṟiya kuṟippurai..
eḷiya naṭaiyilum, paḷḷic ciṟār payaṉmikap peṟum vakaiyilum, tamiḻaṟivu taḻaikkum vakaiyilum innūl amaintuḷḷatu. ciṟuvarkkāṉa kaṭamaikaḷaik kūṟum aṭikaḷār, ciṟu kataikaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyākac cintaṉaikaḷai vaḷart tuḷḷār. aṭikaḷār colvaḷaṉum kaṟpaṉai vaḷaṉum innūṟ ceytikaḷukkut tuṇai niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
āyvu neṟikaḷaiyum āyvup payaṉkaḷaiyum āyvāḷar iyalpukaḷaiyum aṭikaḷār viḷakki yuḷḷār. ulaka nikaḻccikaḷai uṟṟunōkki ārāyum āyvē, uṇmaip payaṉmiku āyvākum.
centamiḻvaḻi ciṟuvarkkāṉa ciṟanta karuttu kaḷai  aṭikaḷār aṉpu vaṭivilum kūṟiyuḷḷār. nalleṇṇamum naṉ muyaṟciyum koṇṭāl celvattaiyum civattiṉ aruḷaiyum koḷ ḷalām eṉak kūṟum innūlukku ārvantūṇṭum aṟivārnta āṅkila muṉṉurai mēlum aḻaku cērkkiṟatu.
- nā. ceyappirakācu
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāriṉ 
ilakkiyap paṭaippukaḷ


Preface
“Tamil can readily dispense with the greater part or whole of Sanscrit, and by dispensing with it rises to a purer and more refined style, where as English cannot abandon its Latin without abandoning perspicuity.:” -Dr. Caldwell in his “Comparative Grammar of the Dravidian Languages,” 3rd edition, P.47.
This great truth discriminatively perceived and impartially proclaimed by Dr. Caldwell the greatest Dravidian philologist, concerning the richness of the Tamil language and the poverty of the other, it is regrettable even Tamil scholars of the land have not sufficiently recognised. How rich, pure and elegant in words and phrases the ancient Tamil literature had been, how simple, yet how lovely, original, edifying and true to nature had the conceptions of its poets and prose writers shone, but to what a poor, mixed, inelegant and low style has the language of its modern literatue fallen, and to what an artificial, ugly, slavish, corrupted and false mode of strain the thoughts and ideas of its authors are fettered, a slight comparison of the two literatures will easily disclose. As has been truly observed by J.A. Symonds, “The genius of a language is the genius of the race which made it.” This significant fact as applying to Tamil cannot be expressed so well as in the notable works of Dr. Gilbert Slater who writes thus: “The very character of the Tamil language, the perfection with which it has been developed into an organ for precise and subtle thought, combined with the fact that it represents a much earlier stage in the evolution of inflexional language than any Indo-Germanic tongue, suggests, though of course it does not prove, the priority of the Dravidians in attaining settled order and regular government.” (The Dravidian Element in Indian Culture, p 70) Though Dr. Slater, in claiming “a greater antiquity for Dravidian than for Aryan civilisation”, only suggests, from a correct understanding of the perfect structure of Tamil, the fact that the Tamilians had attained to a settled order of society and government long before the Aryan people settled into order yet an attentive study of the very ancient Tamil work the Tholkappiam and such old Tamil classics as the Pathuppattu, the Purananuru and others will afford proofs indisputable for substantiating what the learned doctor has hinted above.
As the primary function of language is to facilitate the communication of ideas among a people by means of sounds commonly recognised by them, as the growth of civilisation among them can increase only in proportion as they imbibe the thoughts and ideas of their own inteligent men and make them the guiding principle of life, and as this intermingling of thoughts and ideas cannot take place but through their own recognised, connected, significant sounds called their mother tongue, it had become absolutely necessary for them to preserve their language from constant change and decay and make its usage pure and stable. It is only in the midst of peoples who thus advance in civilisation that the necessity of keeping their tongue pure, undefiled and fixed is deeply felt. Others who take no effort at all to better their condition but are content with the mere satisfaction of their animal appetites, care so little to stamp the usage of their speech that in a round of ten or twenty years a new langauge comes to life among them and in another round it dies away. After quoting many instances to show how languages, like mushrooms, rise and perish rapidly among savages, the great and distinguished linguist Prof. Max Muller says: “We read of missionaries in Central America who attempted to write down the language of savage tribes, and who compiled with great care a dictionary of all the words they could lay hold of. Returning to the same tribe after the lapse of only ten years, they found that this dictionary had become antiquated and useless. Old words had sunk to the ground and new ones had arisen to the surface; an to all outward appearance the language was completely changed.”  (The science of Language, 1899, Vol. I. p66) It must be obvious then how important it is for a civilized people to keep their language intact and maintain its purity to an utmost extent. Still, however civilized a people may be, all the persons who compose it cannot exert themselves to keep their language pure and fixed since laziness in an inborn principle in many which renders them indifferent to a correct pronunciation of words and sentences. And is close association with this principle of laziness which inwardly undermines the structure and stability of a language, another equally baneful, springing from the desire for imitation, comes rather outwardly from the contact of two peoples speaking or using two different languages, and induces them to import the words and phrases of the one into the other. Unless the mischief played by these two evil principles is checked strongly and in time, a language cannot live long but will quickly pass away and with it all scope for culture and civilisation will also pass away.
Now where can we look for a potent power to come from and check the operation of the two evils but from the small but active and intelligent section of a people which forms the centre radiating knowledge and civilisation al around. If a people could be so fortunate, like the Tamilians or the Egyptians, as to possess early among them an intelligent class who, by creating a literature and committing it to writing, made a persistent effort to check the decay of their language and the promiscuous mixture of foreign elements in it, their language, theirs alone, would acquire a vitality that would keep it permanently living, But for the creation and existence of a large and rich literature possessing a many-sided interest for its people, it would not have been possible to arrest the change and decay of a language. Prof. Sayce pertinently well observes: “Destroy literature and facility of intercommunication, and the languages of England and America would soon be as different as those of France and Italy” (The science of Languageï 1900, Vol p 210).
When one grasps the importance of this circumstance which controls the fate and destiny of a language it would become clear to his or her mind why Tamil has lived so long and still continues to live in all its youthful vigour, even after such cultured languages of high antiquity as the Egyptian, the Accadian, the Assyrian, the Hebrew, the Aryan and so on have ceased to exist. So far    as we can compute on historical grounds, it is for more than ten thousand years that the Tamil language is being spoken and written. And to invigorate its constitution in addition to its innate vitality it possesses a vast, varied, original, useful and highly interesting literatue produced from 5000 B.C. up to the present times. The great and momentous fact must be borne in mind that, of the few cultivated languages of the ancient world, Tamil alone, as I just pointed out, is still living in all its richness and youthful vigour, all others of the modern world being only five or six hundred years old and even among these, except some European languages, few own original literatures as pure Tamil.
To many it might look a surprising circumstances that Tamil should outlive all other ancient languages, that it should still display so much virility as argues for its perennial existence. But the secret is not far to seek, although it has eluded the notice of even erudite scholars. The words, phrases and sentences of Tamil require but little effort on the part of the speaker to utter them properly. The sounds of each letter and word issue forth from the throat and mouth normally and naturally, giving no trouble whatever to the speaker. That all its twelve vowels and eighteen consonants constitute the only natural and normal sounds that could come out from the human voice with the least effort, can be shown clearly by inquiring into the phonetic and physiological laws that lie at their basis; but in a short preface like this it is not possible to enter into that profitable study. We have already shown that laziness in pronunciation forms one of the main factors that lead to the constant change, decay and death of languages. But in the case of Tamil, however lazy a man might be, he cannot pronounce its words so badly as to efface their identity altogether. On the other hand, the words themselves, without requiring much effort, flow out from him like the rhythm of a sweet tune issuing forth from the strings of a harp touched by summer breeze. For the Tamil lanugage does not possess such hard consonants, hissing sounds and aspirates as are possessed by Sanscrit, Hebrew and other cognate languages. It is this distinct  melliflous character of its sounds that has preserved and still preserves Tamil from any disastrous change and decay. To this day even the illiterate Tamil peasants understand the classical poetic language of the Thirukkural that was composed at the beginning of the Christian era. When I had been at Jaffna during my lecturing tour, I was struck with wonder when I heard from the lips of its people many pure Tamil words used in ancient Tamil classics, but which have fallen into disuse in Madras and suburban cities. Even big cities like Madras where all sorts of people speaking all sorts of languages mix and mingle together, all those who speak Tamil speak it purely whether they be the born Tamils or others who have adopted it merely for social intercourse or business purposes. In the colloquial Tamil scarcely one percent of either Sanscrit or other foreign words mixes.
But the mischief that affects it badly comes mainly from the circle of English-knowing brahmin and non-brahmin officials, who speak neither pure Tamil nor pure English, but mix the two together so badly and ridiculously that neither a Tamilian nor an Englishman understands their language but turns aside with disgust. Besides introducing into their spoken and written Tamil many an English word and phrase, nay even whole sentences, the brahmins and their followers import into it Sanscrit and Hindustani terms also. This “constant degradation of language” in the words of Pater, “by those who use it carelessly,” at present affects Tamil so wholly by the slipshod fashion in which it is handled by the so called educated people. Whether your speak English, or whether you speak Tamil or any other language, you must learn to use it in such a way as to make your ideas quite intelligible to the people who are born and bred in that language. The standard by which one ought to measure the nature and use of a language must be the extent of its usage among the largest portion of its people in the daily intercourse of their life. As the  educated section forms a very small minority of almost all the people in the world, especially in this country not only is it an inconsiderable part, but it also concerns itself less about the welfare of the masses than its own, the present fashionable mode of their easy talk cannot, with any propriety, be taken as the standard for using a language like Tamil which has been cultivated for more than fifty centuries and is spoken still by more than twentyone millions in Southern India and Ceylon alone. As days advance with the spread  of English education, the gulf between the educated and the uneducated Tamils also widens, their being  little understanding between them and little sympathy with each other. The root-cause of this may, by all careful observers, be traced to the inadequate instruction imparted in Tamil to students reading from the High School to College classes.
The object of the following treatise therefore is to remedy this defect at first by feeding properly the acquisition of the knowledge of Tamil at its very root, and secondly by acquainting the young mind not only with a knowledge of actual facts but also with practical moral maxims clothed, of course, in the attractive allegorical form called the fable.
To attain my first object the whole work is written in pure but simple Tamil, only here and there so interspersed with the words and phrases taken from the classical Tamil literature, as to make their meaning easily understood by their context and help the students in this way early to store in their memory a considerable number of fine and beautiful words and expressions they will frequently meet with in their later course of higher Tamil studies. Although I have in this prose work scrupulously avoided quoting any poetic, yet the synonyms given at the opening of each original essay and the classical terms and expression used here and there, will, I hope, greatly contribute to a sound knowledge of Tamil, of its structure and peculiarities, and aid the student to understand any poetical composition also to some extent. The manner of writing employed in this book, besides increasing the student’s knowledge  of Tamil, will also enrich his Tamil vocabulary and serve him in good stead to communicate easily to his countrymen the ideas he has imbibed from the various branches of English learning.
My second object is to strike out a new path in the treatment of subjects, keeping in view the wide and amazing extension of knowledge achieved by the European nation, whole still clinging to some extent to the ancient mode of classifying subjects as laid down in the great Tamil grammar and rhetoric the Tholkappiam. Almost all the Tamil texts now in use never deviate from the beaten track of repeating the mythical stories of the Ramayana and the Mahabharata, as if the incidents related in them had been actual historical occurrences and of omitting for the most part real daring adventures and soul-inspiring deeds of the heroes who actually existed in countries other than India. By this blind belief and persistent treatment of worn - out myths , by this wanton neglect doggedly maintained towards the actual greatness of the heroes of other countries, these Tamil texts, at the very time of its genial growth, blunt the historic sense of the pupils and breed in its stead an undesirable credulity which never leaves them even after years of their excellent culture in English. I have, therefore, completely left out all kinds of Indian myths in this books and brought in mainly actual historical incidents and a few supernatural events related in the reliable works in English.
In rooting up blind belief and credulity from the mind of the young pupils, it is not my desire that they should be taught to disbelieve completely all occurrences of a wonderful and supernatural character. On the other hand, it is my earnest wish that they should be instructed so as to acquaint themselves early not only with what occur ordinarily within nature and human nature but also with those that occur rarely at fitful intervals within, near and beyond them, provided they belong to the class of cases proved by careful, intelligent and disinterested witnesses. Here too the students must be on their guard, lest they should fall into the error of extending theit belief  from one proved supernatural and superhuman case to many an unproved one. This careful cultivation of mind, while it is plastic, will tend to make it susceptible of receiving into it only those ideas that are true, real and wholesome, and free from deceptions and will conduct it ultimately to and region where truth alone shine. For, greater than truth there is nothing to be attained by the human soul.
Finally, it is my sacred duty to emphasize the importance of diligently cultivating a pure Tamil style both in speaking and writing, for that alone could urge the student to stretch his vision over the vast domain of the very valuable old Tamil literature in search of new words and expressions which lie abundantly there and which will readily serve him to express his precious new ideas in Tamil without seeking the aid of any foreign tongue. The more one exercises his mind in expressing himself clearly in his own mother tongue, the more will his powers of concentration and expression increase, and the better can he serve his country, language and religion. Further, that each and every word of pure Tamil bears on its face the mark of elegant use to which it was put for more than six thousand years by the very intelligent and highly civilized forefathers of the Tamil people, that these words, instead of becoming worn out and unfit for further use like the words of the dead languages, have, on the contrary, gained immense vitality and fresh life as times went on, every patriotic son of Tamil must impress on his mind indelibly. No filial son of Tamil can discard this priceless heritage of his own and go seeking after the aid of others that belong to those who bear no good will towards him, without degrading himself into beggarry and bringing contempt on his great forefathers and on the great language they used and cherished with so much love. Dear sons of Tamil, awake from this negligent torpor, serve your mother with filial piety and sincere heart, and reinstate her once pure and undimmed glory!
Pallavaram,			          Vedachalam
25th March, 1934

1.  pakalavaṉ vaṇakkam
pakalavaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻc col cūriyaṉaik kuṟippatākum. cūriyaṉ eṉṉum col vaṭanāṭṭil irunta āriyar vaḻaṅkiya vaṭacol ākum. itteṉṉāṭṭil uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷ paḻaiya kālattiliruntu pakalavaṉai ñāyiṟu, katiravaṉ, cuṭaravaṉ, pariti, eṉṟūḻ, kaṉali, ellōṉ, veyilōṉ, veyyōṉ mutalāṉa pala centamiḻp peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
irākkālattil tūkkattil irunta nām, pakalavaṉ kiḻakkē tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟkālaiyil kaṇviḻittu eḻuntu, avaṉaip pārkkum pōtu evvaḷavu makiḻcci aṭaintavarkaḷāy niṟkiṉṟōm. kiḻakkē kaṭal maṭṭattiṟku mēl ñāyiṟu vaṭṭamāyp paḷapaḷa eṉṟu varukaiyil aḵtu oru poṉ uruṇṭaiyaip pōl tōṉṟukiṉṟa taṉṟō? appōtu ataṉaic cūḻa atiliruntu pāyum oḷi pallāyirak
kaṇakkāṉa poṟkampikaḷ pōl nīṇṭu cellutalaip pāruṅkaḷ!
irāppoḻutil eṅkum paraviyirunta iruḷait turattik koṇṭu katiravaṉ vāṉattiṉmēl eḻumpum intak kāṭciyai nām kāṇkaiyil, atu civanta tirumēṉi uṭaiyaṉākiya murukappirāṉ, kaṟutta uruvattiṉai uṭaiya arakkar kūṭṭattiṉ mēl pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa taṉ ampukaḷai ēvi avarkaḷait turattik koṇṭu varuvatu
pōl tōṉṟukiṉṟataṉṟō? mikunta oḷiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum accuṭaravaṉukkuk kīḻē paccai niṟattuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭum kaṭalāṉatu ammurukappirāṉ ēṟiyirukkum paccaimayil pōlavum, akkaṭalil eḻuntu eḻuntu kīḻē viḻuntu ulāvum alaikaḷāṉavai anta mayil iṟakkaikaḷai virittu aṭittup paṟantu varuvatu pōlavum tōṉṟutalai nōkkuṅkaḷ!
iṅṅaṉamāka, appariti vāṉavaṉatu tōṟṟam, oḷivaṭivāṉa kaṭavuḷiṉ tōṟṟattaiyē ottirukkiṉṟatu. atu allāmalum, akkaṭavuḷ ellā uyirkaḷaiyum aṟiyāmai eṉṉum iruḷiṉiṉṟum viṭuvittu, avaṟṟiṉ aṟivai viḷaṅkac ceytal pōla, iv eṉṟūḻum uṟakkattilirunta ellā uyirkaḷiṉ aṟivaiyum eḻuppi, avaikaḷaip palavakai muyaṟcikaḷil ēvukiṉṟatu pāruṅkaḷ! kaṉali tōṉṟukiṟa viṭiyaṟkālattil kōḻi kūvukiṉṟatu, kākkai karaikiṉṟatu, māntōppukaḷiṉ ucci
yilē kuyiliṉ iṉiya oli kēṭkiṟaṉtu. iṉṉum ettaṉaiyō paṟavaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ ettaṉaiyō vakaiyāṉa ōcaikaḷum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. iravellām koṭṭilil kiṭanta ākkaḷ (pacukkaḷ)  tokuti tokutiyāka veḷi nilaṅkaḷil pulmēyap pōkiṉṟaṉa. uḻavarkaḷ erutukaḷai ēriṟpūṭṭi vayalkaḷai uḻat tuvaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
iravu muḻuvatum tūkkamayakkattuṭaṉ carakkuvaṇṭikaḷ ōṭṭi vanta vaṇṭikkārarkaḷ accarakkup potikaḷaip paṇṭacālai muṟṟaṅkaḷil iṟakkukiṉṟaṉar. palvakaip poruḷkaḷai viṟkum kaṭaikkārarkaḷ ellām tattam kaṭaikaḷai varicai varicaiyākat tiṟantu vaittu, avaikaḷai vāṅka varuvārkku viṟpaṉai ceykiṉṟaṉar. palvakaik kaittoḻilāḷarkaḷum tattam toḻiṟcālaikaḷukkuc ceṉṟu tāmtām ceyyum toḻilkaḷaic ceyyat tuvaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. araciyal toḻil pārppavar tattam araciyal nilaiyaṅkaḷukkuc cellutalaiyum, paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷil kalvi payilum ciṟuvarkaḷum, kallūrikaḷil, palkalai payilum iḷaiñarkaḷum tattam kaḻakaṅkaḷukku ēkutalaiyum pāruṅkaḷ! kaṭavuḷ eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum tirukkōyilkaḷil ellām katavu tiṟantu kurukkaḷmār kālai vaḻipāṭu ceyvatu evvaḷavu ciṟanta makiḻcciyiṉait tarukiṉṟatu! iṅṅaṉamellām naṭaipeṟum uyirkaḷiṉ ittaṉai muyaṟcikaḷukkum kāraṇamāvatu ellōṉatu tōṟṟamē eṉpatu naṉṟāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō?
itumaṭṭumō! namatu uyirvāḻkkaikkuk, kaṭṭāyamāka vēṇṭappaṭum nel, kōtumai, payaṟu, paccaikkāykaṟi mutaliyavaikaḷai nām viḷaittuk koḷḷutaṟkum veyilōṉatu veyiloḷiyē kāraṇamāyirukkiṉṟatu. veyil veḷiccam paṭāviṭṭāl entap puṟpūṇṭum, enta marañceṭi koṭiyum cevvaiyāka vaḷarntu payaṉ taramāṭṭā. iṉṉum, maḻaikkāla, paṉikkālaṅkaḷil namatu uṭampu kuḷirāl viṟaittup pōkāmalum, nām puḻaṅkum taṇṇīr paṉik kaṭṭiyāka iṟukip pōkāmal iruppataṟkum veyyōṉatu veppam mutaṉyaika vēṇṭiyirukkiṟa taṉṟō?
nām irukkum iṭaṅkaḷellām paṉimūṭip pōkumāṉāl, nām entap paṇṭattaiyāvatu payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷa muṭiyumā? namatuṭampu viṟaittup pōkumāṉāl nām uyirōṭiruttal iyalumā? iyalātē? ātalāl, nām namatu uyir vāḻkkaiyaic cevvaṉē naṭaipeṟuvittaṟkum, namatu vāḻkkaikkuk kaṭṭāyamāka vēṇṭappaṭum ellāp paṇṭaṅkaḷum namakkup payaṉpaṭum nilaiyiliruntu utavu vataṟkum veyyōṉuṭaiya oḷiyum veppamum mutaṉmaiyāka iruttalai nām karuttiṟ patittuk koḷvōmāka!
ivvāṟu, ellā uyirkaḷukkum aṟivaiyum muyaṟciyaiyum eḻupputalilum, avvuyirkaḷai uṭampukaḷil tōṉṟac ceytu avaṟṟaip pātukāttalilum, avvuyirkaḷ nukarccikku vēṇṭiya ellā uṇavup poruḷkaḷaiyum viḷaittut tarutalilum pakalavaṉuṭaiya taṉmaiyum ceyalum kaṭavuḷiṉ taṉmaiyaiyum aruṭceyalaiyum ottiruttalāl, appakalavaṉaiyē kaṭavuḷāka allatu kaṭavuḷiṉ oḷi vaṭivākak karutik, kālaiyil avaṉ kiḻakkil eḻumpōtum, mālaiyil avaṉ mēṟkil maṟaiyum pōtum avaṉai uḷḷam urukik kaikūppit toḻuvōmāka!
2. ulaka iyakkam
nām uṟaiyum ivvulakamāṉatu vāṉ veḷiyil pantu pōl cuḻalum ōr uruṇṭai vaṭiviṉatākum. nām irukkum itaṉ mēṟparappiṉ maiyattiliruntu itaṉai ūṭuruvit tuḷaittuk koṇṭu aḷantapaṭiyē nām itaṉ kīḻuḷḷa mēṟparappiṉ maiyattiṟ ceṉṟu cērak kūṭumāyiṉ, iḵtu 7,900 mail kuṟukkaḷavuḷḷatāy iruttalai aṟintu koḷḷalām. itaṉ cuṟṟaḷavō 25,000 mailākum.
iḵtu uruṇṭai vaṭiviṉateṉpatu eṅṅaṉam aṟiyak kūṭumeṉiṉ, nām oru kaṭaṟkaraiyiṟ ceṉṟu niṉṟu, akkaraiyai nōkki varum oru kappalaik kāṇkuvamāyiṉ, neṭuntolaiviliruntu varum akkappaliṉ mēluḷḷa pāymaraṅkaḷē mutaṉmutaṉ kaṭpulaṉākum. piṉṉar atu innilaccarivil paravi niṟkum kaṭalmēl ēṟik kiṭṭa varavarap pāymarattiṉ kīḻuḷḷa ataṉ muḻuvuṭampum paiyappaiya naṉku pulaṉāyt tōṉṟum. aṅṅaṉamē, nām niṟkum karaiyiṉ tuṟaimukattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭut tolaiviṟ cellum oru marakkalamāṉatu kaṭalnīr cariviṉ kīḻ iṟaṅka iṟaṅka ataṉ muḻuvuṭampum ciṟituciṟitāy maṟaintupōka, ataṉ mēluḷḷa pāymaraṅkaḷē piṉṉum ciṟitu nēram varaiyil kaṭpulaṉā kiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉum piṉṉum atu kīḻ iṟaṅka ataṉ pāy maraṅkaḷum maṟaintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl ivvulaka māṉatu uruṇṭai vaṭiviṉatātal peṟappaṭukiṉṟataṉṟō? iṉṉum namatu ilaṅkait tīviṉ teṉmuṉaik kaṇṇatāṉa māttaḷai yiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuk kiḻakku nōkkiyē cellum oru nāvāyāṉatu, mīṇṭum ammāttaḷait tuṟaimukat tilēyē vantu cērak kāṇṭalālum innilavulakam uruṇṭai vaṭiviṉateṉpatu viḷaṅkā niṟkiṉṟatu. itaṟku oru pantiṉ naṭuvil uḷḷa oru puḷḷiyiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu nērē cellum ōr eṟumpu tirumpavum appuḷḷiyilēyē vantu cērtalai eṭuttuk kāṭṭalām. atuvēyumaṉṟit, tiṅkaḷ maṟaivu (cantirakirakaṇa) kālattil, ataṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum ivvulakattiṉ niḻalāṉatu vaṭṭavaṭiviṉatāyk kāṇappaṭuva tālum iḵtu uruṇṭai vaṭiviṉatātal teḷiyappaṭum.
avvāṟāyiṉ pantupōl uruṇṭirukkum innilamaṇṭilam vāṉveḷiyil ētoru paṟṟukkōṭum iṉṟic cuḻaṉṟu celvatu oru perum putumaiyākat tōṉṟu
kiṉṟataṉṟō? eṉiṉ; ām; ārāyntu pārātavarkku atu putumaitāṉ. ārāyntu pārppavarkkō, ataṉ uṇmai naṉku viḷaṅkum. ciṟumiyar iruvar etir etirē niṉṟu tamkaikaḷaik kōttup piṭittuk koṇṭu piṉṉē cāynta vaṇṇamāyt tumpi cuṟṟutalaip pāruṅkaḷ! aṅṅaṉam cuṟṟuṅkāl piṉṉē cāyntirukkum avviruvaruṅ kīḻ viḻātatu ēṉ? avarkaḷ tam kaikaḷai nīṭṭi iṟukap piṭittirut talālaṉṟō? atu pōlavē, pakalavaṉ maṇṭilamāṉatu innilavulakattai iḻukka, iḵtu ataṉai iḻukka, ivvāṟṟal oṉṟu maṟṟaiyatai viṭṭup piriyātāy vāṉveḷiyiṟ cuḻaṉṟu celkiṉṟateṉka. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, aṟivillāta ivviruvēṟu ulakum aṟivuṭaiya ciṟumakāraippōl oṉṟaiyoṉṟu iḻuttuk koṇṭu cellutal yāṅṅaṉa meṉiṉ, aṟivuṭaiya uyirkaḷ maṭṭumē oṉṟaiyoṉṟu iḻuppaṉa, maṟṟaiya iḻā eṉṟu koḷḷutal poruntātu. aṟivillāta poruḷkaḷum oṉṟaiyoṉṟu iḻukka vallaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. oru kāntakkal taṉ parumaṉukku ēṟpac ciṟiya periya irumput tuṇṭukaḷai iḻuttal kāṇalām. akkāntak kallai oru kaṭitattiṉ kīḻ piṭittu, akkaṭitattiṉ mēl cila ūcikaḷai vaittuk kīḻuḷḷa kāntak kallai neṭuka iḻuttāl mēluḷḷa irumpūcikaḷum, ataṉāl iḻukkap paṭṭu, akkaṭitattiṉ mēl nakarutalaip pārkkalām. aṟivillāta kāntam iṅṅaṉam iḻukkum āṟṟal vāyntatāy iruttal pōlavē, aṟivillāta ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilamum, vāṉaveḷiyiṉūṭu innilavulakattai iḻukka, ituvum ataṉaiyiḻukka iraṇṭum iṭaiveḷiyiṟ cuḻaṉṟu celkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu ōrntukoḷka.
irākkālattil namakku nilavoḷiyiṉait tarum tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilamum innilavulakattāl iḻukkappaṭṭu, itaṉaic cuṟṟiyapaṭiyē ñāyiṟṟiṉaiyum cuṟṟic celkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē, cevvāy, putaṉ, viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi mutalāṉa ulakaṅkaḷum ñāyiṟṟiṉai iḻukka, atu maṟṟu ivvulakaṅkaḷ ellā vaṟṟaiyum oruṅkē iḻukka, ivai tāmum tammuḷ oṉṟai yoṉṟu caṉṉal piṉṉalāy iḻukka ellām vāṉ veḷiyiṟ paṟpala pantukaḷ pōl ñāyiṟṟiṉaic cūḻntu cellā niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
aṟṟēl, maṟṟai āṟu maṇṭilaṅkaḷum ñāyiṟṟiṉaic cūḻntu celkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu koḷḷutal eṉṉaiyeṉil; ivvēḻu ulakaṅkaḷilum, ñāyiṟu oṉṟumē mikapperiyatu. nāmirukkum innilavulakattaiviṭa ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilam paṉṉiraṇṭu nūṟāyiram maṭaṅku periyatāy irukkiṉṟatu. ataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyākap periyatāy uḷḷatu viyāḻa maṇṭilamē ākum; ivviyāḻaṉ namatu maṇṇulakattiṉum ōrāyirattu nāṉūṟumaṭaṅku periyatu. itaṟku aṭuttapaṭi periyatu caṉi maṇṭilam. itaṟkuc ciṟiyatu namatu maṇṇulaku. namatu maṇṇulakukkum ciṟiyatu veḷḷiyulaku. ataṟkum ciṟiyatu cevvāy ulaku. ataṟkum ciṟiyatu putaṉulakākum. iṅṅaṉam maṟṟai āṟum, pakal maṇṭilattiṟkuc ciṟiyaṉavāy iruttalāl, ciṟiyavākiya avai periyatākiya pakalulakiṉāl īrkkappaṭṭu ataṉaic cūḻntu ōṭiyapaṭiyāy ulavukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṇarntu koḷka.
iṉi, murukappirāṉukku nērttikkaṭaṉ celuttum toṇṭar cilar, avaṉ eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum tirukkōyilaic cuṟṟi nilattiṟkiṭantu puraṇṭukoṇṭē valam varutalai nīṅkaḷ pārttirukkalām. aṅṅaṉam puraṇṭu valam varupavar mutalil tammaittām cuṟṟip piṉṉar akkōyilaic cuṟṟi valam varutal pōlavē, ivvulakamum mutalil taṉṉait tāṉ cuṟṟip piṟaku ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉaic cuṟṟi varukiṉṟatu.
iḵtu orumuṟai taṉṉaittāṉ cuṟṟutalukku irupattu nāṉku maṇi nēram piṭikkiṉṟatu. mēṟkiliruntu kiḻakku mukamāy iḵtu iṅṅaṉam taṉṉaittāṉ cuṟṟuṅkāl ñāyiṟṟiṉai nōkki iyaṅkum itaṉ orupāti pakal veḷiccam uṭaiyatākavum, ñāyiṟṟiṉ mukamāyt tirumpāta ataṉ maṟupāti iruṇṭa allatu nilavoḷi keḻumiya irāppoḻutu uṭaiyatāyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nām uṟaiyum itteṉtamiḻnāṭṭil pakalavaṉ ucciyil viḷaṅkum vēḷai naṇpakaṟ poḻutāyirukkaiyil, namakkunērē kīḻ uḷḷa amerikkā tēyam naḷḷirāp poḻutu vāyntatāy irukkiṉṟatu; namatu tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkum amerikkā tēyattiṟkum naṭuvil uḷḷatākiya iṅkilāntu tēyamō ap
pōtu viṭiyaṟkālam uṭaiyatāyt tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
ivvāṟāka ivvulakam irupattunāṉku maṇinērattil taṉṉaittāṉ cuṟṟiyapaṭiyāy, taṉṉilum paṉṉūṟāyira maṭaṅku peritāṉa pakalavaṉ maṇṭilattai muṟṟuñ cuṟṟi varuvataṟku munnūṟṟu aṟupattaintēkāl nāṭkaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam itu orumuṟai katiravaṉaic cuṟṟi varuṅkālamē ōr āṇṭu (vaṭamoḻiyil varuṣam) eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam veyyavaṉ ulakiṉaic cuṟṟic cuḻaṉṟu varutal uṇmaiyāyiṉ, itaṉ iyakkam namakkup pulaṉākāmaiyum, pakalavaṉē itaṉaic cuṟṟi varupavaṉākap pulappaṭutalum eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ; iḵtu oru maṇinērattil āyiram mail viḻukkāṭu katiravaṉ maṇṭilattai miku viraivākac cūḻntu cuḻalutalāl, attuṇai viraivākac cellum itaṉkaṇ uyirvāḻum namakku itaṉ iyakkam pulaṉākātu pōka, itaṉāṟ cuṟṟappaṭum pakalavaṉē itaṉaic cuṟṟi varupavaṉpōl pulappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. oru maṇikku muppatu mail viḻukkāṭu ōṭum pukaivaṇṭiyil cellum nammaṉōrkkē appukai vaṇṭiyiṉ ōṭṭam pulappaṭāmal ataṉ irupakkattum niṟkum nilaṉum, marañceṭi koṭikaḷumē ōṭuvatu pōl pulappaṭumāyiṉ, niṉaittaṟkum ariya kaṭuviraivuṭaṉ orumaṇi nērattiṟku āyirammail viḻukkāṭu cuḻaṉṟōṭum innilavulakiṉ iyakkam namakkut teṉpaṭumō colluṅkaḷ! ākavē, nilaiyil uḷḷatu pōl kāṇap paṭuvatu paṟṟi immaṇṇulaku nilam eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkappaṭiṉum, ārāyntu pārkkuṅkāl vāṉakkaṭalil itu mikutiyum viraintu ōṭum ōr ōṭamākavē kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu eṉka.
iṉi, innilavulakattiṉ iyakkattil vēṟoru mutaṉmaiyāṉa taṉmaiyum uḷatu. itu teṟku vaṭakkil ceṅkuttāy niṉṟapaṭi cuḻalāmal taṉ parumaṉ 360 paṅkil ciṟitēṟakkuṟaiyap patiṉaintu paṅku cāyntapaṭiyāy ñāyiṟṟiṉaic cuṟṟic cuḻaṉṟu celkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam cāyntu cuḻalvataṉāl itaṉ vaṭamuṉai teṉmuṉaikaḷ āṟu tiṅkaḷ pakalavaṉ mukamāy tirumpiyum piṉ āṟu tiṅkaḷ pakalavaṉai viṭṭut tirumpiyum cuḻalkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl avviru muṉaikaḷil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷ āṟu tiṅkaḷ oḷiyum piṉ āṟu tiṅkaḷ iruḷum vāyntaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamuṉaiyaic cārnta nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇatāṉa mērumalaiyaic cūḻnta pakutikaḷil āṟu tiṅkaḷ katiravaṉ oḷiyum, maṟṟai āṟu tiṅkaḷ avaṉ aṅkuk kāṇappaṭāmaiyiṉ iruḷum toṭarpāy irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu māpārata vaṉaparvam (163, 37, 38) pakarvatum namatu nilavulakiṉ cāynta iyakkattāl avviru muṉaikaḷil kāṇappaṭum avviruvakai nikaḻccikaḷaiyē kuṟippikkiṉṟatu. ātaliṉāl tāṉ, vaṭamuṉaiyil uṟaiyum makkaḷukku āṟu tiṅkaḷ oru pakaṟpoḻutāyum, maṟṟai āṟu tiṅkaḷ ōr irāppoḻutāyum iṅkuḷḷavarāl koḷḷap paṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭatēyaṅkaḷil uṟaiyum makkaḷait tēvarkaḷākak koḷḷutal itteṉṉāṭṭavarkku vaḻakkam. ātalāl, pakaloḷi vīcum āṟu tiṅkaḷaiyum, attēvarkaṭku oru pakaṟ poḻutākavum, avvoḷiyiṉṟi iruḷcūḻnta ēṉai āṟu tiṅkaḷaiyum avarkaṭku ōr irāppoḻutākavum innāṭṭavar paṇṭutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. innuṭpaṅkaḷ ellām paṇṭai āriyamakkaḷiṉ paḻaiya uṟaiyuḷai naṉkārāynta pālakaṅkātara tilakarāl virivāka eṭuttu viḷakkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamuṉaiyil uḷḷārkku ovvōrāṇṭilum pakalavaṉoḷi mutaṉmutal kaṭpulaṉāy tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟkālam paṅkuṉit tiṅkaḷ oṉpatām nāḷ tuvaṅkukiṉṟatu. atiliruntu āṟu tiṅkaḷ varaiyil cuṭaravaṉ oḷi vāṉiṉ kaṇ tulaṅkiya paṭiyāy iruntu puraṭṭācit tiṅkaḷ eṭṭām nāḷ maṟaintu pōka, atiliruntu maṟu paṅkuṉi varaiyil avvaṭanāṭukaḷ iruḷ cūḻap paṭṭaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉam iruḷ cūḻntiruntālum, pakalavaṉ tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉ aṟikuṟiyākak kāṇappaṭum vaikaṟaip poḻutu paṅkuṉikku mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷ muṉṉarē mārkaḻit tuvakkattil annāṭṭavar kaṇkaṭkup pulaṉākutalāltāṉitteṉṉāṭṭavarkaḷ tēvarkaṭkup poḻutu viṭiyum kālamāka mārkaḻit tiṅkaḷaik koṇṭāṭi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṟu tiṅkaḷ iruḷil kiṭantu mūḻkum makkaṭku oru vakaiyil āṟutalum makiḻcciyum tarum poruṭṭu, iṟaivaṉ avvaṭamuṉai nāṭukaḷukku oru peru miṉṉal oḷiyiṉaic ciṟitukālam varaiyil vāṉiṉkaṇ tōṟṟuvittu varutalaiyum ciṟārkaḷ karuttiṟ patittu, iṟaivaṉatu pēraruḷ tiṟattai viyantu vāḻttuvārāka!
3. kuṟiñci-malai
nām uyirvāḻum inta nila ulakamāṉatu, malaiyum, kaṭalum, kāṭum, nāṭum eṉa nāṉku pakutikaḷāka nammuṭaiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. innāṉkum allāta veṟu maṇalveḷiyum uṇṭu. malaiyum malaicārnta iṭamum kuṟiñciyeṉṟu collappaṭum.
malai eṉṉum collukkup poruḷ valimaiyiṉai uṭaiyatu. mal eṉṟāl valimai. malaikaḷellām perumpālum valivāṉa karuṅkallāl ākkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. mikavum uyaramāka irukkum malaiyai ōṅkal, piṟaṅkal, poruppu, veṟpu, eṉṟum, ōriṭattil kuṟukkē vaḷarntu nīṇṭu kiṭakkum malaiyai vilaṅkal eṉṟum, oṉṟaṉ mēloṉṟu aṭukkuaṭukkāy vaḷarntirukkum malaiyai aṭukkal eṉṟum, etiroli ceyyum malaiyaic cilampu eṉṟum, mūṅkiṟkāṭukaḷ uḷḷa malaiyai varai eṉṟum, kāṭukaḷ aṭarnta malaiyai iṟumpu eṉṟum, ciṟiya malaiyaik kuṉṟu, kuvaṭu, kuṟumpoṟai eṉṟum, maṇ mikunta malaiyaip poṟṟai, poccai eṉṟum, malaip pakkattaic cāral eṉṟum paḻaiya tamiḻnūlkaḷ kūṟā niṟkum.
malaikaḷiṉ iyaṟkai aḻakaik kāṇavēṇṭumāṉāl teṟkē tirukkuṟṟālam, pāvanācam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa malaikaḷaiyum kiḻakkē kīḻkkaṇavāy malait toṭaril uḷḷa cērvarāyaṉ malaiyaiyum, naṭuvē nīlakiri malaiyaiyum, mēṟkē puṉalūr pakkamāy uḷḷa malaiyāḷattaiyum, vaṭakkē imayamalaiyaiyum pōyp pārttal vēṇṭum.
teṟkēyuḷḷa pāvanāca malaiyaṭivārattil taṉiniṟkum civapirāṉ tirukkōyilum, ataṉ valappuṟattum etirilum ōṭivarum porunaiyāṟum (tāmiraparuṇi) avvāṟṟaṅ
karaiyil vāṉ aḷāvi niṟkum maraṅkaḷum, avvāṟṟaṅkaraiyiṉ appakkattē neṭukattōṉṟum malaittoṭariṉ ōriṭattil mukaṭu civanta ōṭukaḷ vēyntu uṭampellām veṇmaiyāka amaikkappaṭṭut tōṉṟum pañcālaik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum, kōyiliṉ piṉpuṟattē avvāṟṟiṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum maṇṭapaṅkaḷum, ammalait
toṭar eṅkum uḷḷa aṭarnta kāṭukaḷum putitu ceṉṟu pārppavarkaḷukku avar muṉṉaṟiyāta ōr iṉpavuṇarviṉai ūṭṭi avaruḷḷattaik kiḷarac ceykiṉṟaṉa.
vaikācit tiṅkaḷ muṭivil aṅkēceṉṟu, attirukkōyililiruntu oru nāḻikai vaḻi naṭantu ceṉṟāl, akattiyar kōyiloṉṟu kāṇappaṭum. malaip pakkatti
liruntu virinta veḷḷāṭai toṅka viṭṭāṟ pōlak kumukumu eṉṟa ōcaiyuṭaṉ kīḻviḻum aruviyiṉ kāṭci ettakaiyavar uḷḷattaiyum kavarum taṉmaiyatāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avvaruvinīr kīḻviḻum iṭattil mika āḻntatoru maṭu amaintuḷḷatu. ammaṭuvukkuc cellum ōr oṟṟaiyaṭippātai ceṅkuttāṉa malaiccuvarai aṭuttup pōkiṉṟatu. appātaiyil celluṅkāl, kīḻ iṟaṅkum aruvi nīrt tivalaiyum cāraṟkāṟṟum celvōriṉ mukattilum uṭampilum etirttu aṟaivatu pōṟpaṭum; appōtu kaṇviḻittup pārttālum varuttamāyirukkum. eṉṟālum, ataṟku añcātu etirēṟic ceṉṟu, ammaṭuvil mikavum viḻippāy iṟaṅki muḻukiṉāl, mikak kuḷirnta annīriṉ muḻukku mikkatoru maṉakkaḷippiṉaiyum āṟutalaiyum tarum; uṭampiṉ nalattiṟkum mika ēṟṟatu.
avvaḷavu naṭantu ceṉṟu, ammaṭuvil muḻuka iyalātavarkaḷ, ammaṭuvil niṟaintu vaḻintu, mēṟcoṉṉa akattiyar kōyil pakkamāy viraintu ōṭivarum porunai
yāṟṟilēyē nīrāṭalām. āṉāl, avviṭattil avvāṟu āḻam iṉṟi iṭuppaḷaviṟkum kuṟainta nīr uṭaiyatāy ēkutalāl, atil paṭuttapaṭiyāyiruntu muḻukutalē kūṭum. aṅṅaṉam muḻukuṅkāl viḻippāyirukka vēṇṭum. ēṉeṉil avvāṟṟiṉ nīr malaimēliruntu kīḻviḻuntu miku viraivāy celvataṉāl, āḷai nilaikkaviṭāmal puraṭṭit taḷḷik koṇṭu pōyviṭum. mēlum, avyāṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭai yeṅkum oruperum pūcaṉikkāy parumaṉ uḷḷa uruṇṭaik kaṟkaḷ niṟaintirukkiṉṟaṉa! ataṉāl, ataṉ aṭiyiṟ kālaippatiyavaittu niṟṟalum muṭiyātu; ciṟitu picaki viḻuntālum puraṇṭālum maṇṭai aṭipaṭum; uṭampum kālkaikaḷum avaṟṟil uraicik kāyappaṭum. āṉālum, viḻippāyiruntu nīrāṭupavarkkup paḷiṅkupōl teḷintu ōṭum ataṉnīr mēṉimēṟ kuḷukuḷuveṉṟupaṭṭup pēriṉimai yiṉait tarum.
nīrāṭiyapiṉ avvāṟṟaṅ karaiyiṉiṭattē irukkum akattiyar kōyilil ceṉṟu iṟaivaṉai vaṇaṅkuvatu uḷḷattaip pēr aruḷ iṉpattil amiḻttum. cuṟṟi vāṉōṅkiya malaikaḷum kāṭukaḷum aṭarntirukka, etirē uḷḷatoru malai mukaṭṭiliruntu pāl oḻukuvatu pōl ōveṉṟa iraiccaluṭaṉ aruvi nīr viḻa, viḻunta annīr tiraṇṭa kaṟkaḷiṉ mēl viraintōṭic cella, aṅṅaṉam annīr cellum porunai āṟṟaṅkaraimēl avakattiyar kōyil taṉimaiyil niṟka. ataṉuḷ oru kurukkaḷ vaḻipāṭu ceyyumpōtu, aṅkē vaṇaṅkac celvōr maṉam, ulaka niṉaivukaḷai viṭṭu, aṅkuḷḷa iyaṟkaik kāṭcikaḷiṉ aḻakāl mēl untappaṭṭu, uyirkaḷiṉ taṉitta tuṇaiyaṟṟa nilaimaiyiṉaiyum, annilaimaiyil iṟaivaṉ oruvaṉē avvuyirkaṭku oppaṟṟa tuṇaivaṉāy vāyttuḷḷa arumaiyiṉaiyum niṉaintu niṉaintu nekkuviṭṭu urukukiṉṟatu.
ippāvanācak kāṭciyiṉai oppavē, tirukkuṟṟāla malai aṭivārattilum periyatoru paḻaiya civapirāṉ tirukkōyil amaintuḷḷatu. ataṉ arukēyum, malai
mēliruntu viḻum periyatōr aruvi vīḻcci kāṇṭaṟku mika iṉiyatōr aḻakiṉai uṭaittāyt tikaḻkiṉṟatu. vēṉiṟ kāla naṭuvil ivvaruviyil talaimuḻukutaṟ poruṭṭup pallāyirakkaṇakkiṉarāṉa makkaḷ itteṉṉāṭu eṅkaṇu miruntu vantu tiraḷkiṉṟaṉar.
ittirukkuṟṟāla malaiyiṉ aṭivārattiliruntu mēlē cellutaṟkut tiruttamāṉa akaṉṟatorupātai amaikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. itu vaḷaintu mēlē caṇpakāṭavi varaiyil celkiṉṟatu. itaṉūṭu celkaiyil itaṉ irupuṟattum ōṅki vaḷarntirukkum aṭarnta marakkāṭukaḷiṉ kāṭci maṉattiṟku accattōṭu kalanta oru makiḻcciyiṉait tōṟṟuvikkum. caṇpakāṭaviyiṉ arukē aḻakiyatoru maṇṭapam amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; aṅkē aruvi viḻum iṭattiṉ pakkattē oru kukaiyum uḷatu; irupatu āṇṭu
kaḷukkumuṉ yām aṅkē ceṉṟapōtu iḷamparuvattiṉarāṉa oru tuṟavi akkukaiyil taṅkiyiruntaṉar. emmaik kaṇṭatum mukam malarntu varavēṟṟut, tām irukkum akkukaiyiṉ uḷḷē emmai aḻaittu, ataṉ uḷ amaippai emakkuk kāṭṭiṉār.
iruperuṅ kaṟpāṟaikaḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟu toṭutalāl aḵtu oru ciṟiya aṟaipōl iyaṟkaiyamaippu vāyntiruntatu. ataṉuḷ uyaramāṉa oruvar nērāka niṟṟal iyalātu. kuṉintapaṭiyākac ceṉṟutāṉ irukkavēṇṭum. nāṉku pēr irukkavum, iruvar paṭukkavum pōtumāṉa iṭantāṉ uḷatu. attuṟavi akkukai vāyilil ciṟu ceṅkal cuvar eḻuppi atil oru ciṟu katavu porutti iruntār. veḷikkāṟṟu uḷḷē nuḻaivataṟkāka akkataviṉ mēṟ pakutiyil ciṟuciṟutuḷaikaḷ uṇṭu. irākkālattil aruvi nīr kuṭikkavarum yāṉai, puli, ciṟuttai, kāṭṭuppaṉṟi mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷ urumum ōcaiyum avai oṉṟai yoṉṟu ciṉantu tākkic ceyyum iraiccalum perun tikilai viḷaikkumeṉṟum, nilākkālattil akkataviṉ tuḷaivaḻiyē tām avvilaṅkukaḷiṉ vaṭivattaip pārppatu uṇṭeṉṟum avar emakkuk kūṟiṉār. kīḻē malaiyaṭi vārattil viḻum aruviyaip pārkkilum mēlē caṇpakāṭaviyil viḻum aruvinīr kuḷirntatāy irukkiṉṟatu; taṇṇīriṉ aḷavum ciṟitu kuṟaintatāka iruttalāl talaimēlum uṭampiṉ mēlum ataṉait tāṅki muḻukuvatu iṅkē eḷitāyum mika iṉitāyum irukkum.
iṉic caṇpakāṭaviyait tāṇṭi, iṉṉum mēlēṟi uyarattiluḷḷa tēṉaruvikkuc cellat tiruttamāṉa akaṉṟa pātai illai. iṇṭu iṭukkut teriyāmal aṭarn
tirukkum peruṅkāṭukaḷiṉ ūṭē ōr oṟṟaiyaṭip pātaitāṉ celkiṉṟatu. appātai neṭuka utirnta ilaic cettaikaḷum, vērkaḷum, koṭikaḷum nirampiyiruttalāl avaṟṟiṉmēl naṭappatu accantarum; iṭaiyiṭaiyē ciṟuciṟu malaippiḷavukaḷum uṇṭu; āṉālum, avaikaḷai eḷitil tāṇṭic cellalām. tiṭīreṉṟu, yāṉai, vēṅkaip puli, muḷḷampaṉṟi mutalāṉa maṟavilaṅkukaḷ vantu kuṟukkiṭutal uṇṭākaiyāl, mēlē tēṉaruvikkuc celvōr oruvar iruvarāy pōtal kūṭātu. aintāṟu pērkkuk kuṟaiyāmaṟ kaiyil taṭikkampu tāṅki iraiccal iṭṭukkoṇṭē pakal vēḷaiyiṟ cellavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ceṉṟāl tēṉaruviyaip pōy aṭaiyalām. aṭaintatum aṅkuḷḷa kāṭci kaṇṇum maṉamum kavarntu mēl ōṅki viḷaṅkutal aṟiyappaṭum. aṅkē viḻum tēṉaruviyāṉatu mikavum ceṅkuttāṉa oru malaic cuvariṉ mēliruntu vaḻikiṉṟatu. av varuvic cuvaruṭaṉ valappuṟattil iṇaintirukkum kaṟpāṟai, mēlē kuṭaikavittāṟ pōl amaintuḷḷatu; mēliruntu tēṉaruvi vaḻiyum mūlaiyil nālaintu teṉṉaimara uyarattil vaṭṭamāṉa oru peruntēṉaṭai iruntatu. avvaḷavu periya tēṉ aṭaiyai yām vēṟu eṅkumē kaṇṭatillai. ataṉ kuṟukkaḷavu ciṟitu ēṟakkuṟaiyap pattu aṭi irukkum. ataṉ niṟañ ciṟu civappāy iruntamaiyāl, atu kālaiyil eḻum pakalavaṉ vaṭivai ottiruntatu. attēṉaṭaiyiṉ pakkattuḷḷa mūlai mēliruntu aruvinīr vīḻtal paṟṟiyē ataṉait tēṉaruviyeṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum, ek kālattumē attēṉaṭai aṅku irukkum eṉṟum emmuṭaṉ vantār collak kēṭṭōm.
tēṉaruvi kīḻviḻum iṭam āḻnta oru ciṟu kuṭṭamāyirukkiṟatu. mēliruntu viḻum nīriṉ kaṭumaiyāl akkuṭṭam nuraiyum kumiḻiyum uṭaiyatāy ōyātu niṟaintu taṉṉiliruntu maṟṟoru ciṟṟaruviyiṉai uṇṭākka, acciṟṟaruviyē varavarap peritākik, kīḻē caṇpakāṭaviyilum, ataṟkum kīḻē tirukkuṟṟālap perumāṉ kōyiliṉ pakkattum pōy vīḻkiṉṟatu. tēṉaruviyiṉ kīḻ uḷḷa kuṭṭam oru kaṟpāṟaiyiṉ kuṭaiyil amaintiruttalāl, ataṉkaṇ nirampum nīr mikka teḷivuṭaiyatāy tūytāy mikak kuḷirntu irukkiṉṟatu.
4. mullai-kāṭu
maraṅkaḷ aṭarnta nilappakutiyē perumpālum kāṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. kāṭum kāṭu cārnta iṭamum mullaiyeṉap peyar peṟumeṉṟu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟāniṟkum. paruttuyarnta maraṅkaḷ neruṅkiya kāṭṭai vallai eṉṟum, ciṟu maraṅkaḷ miṭainta kāṭṭai iṟumpu, kuṟuṅkāṭu eṉṟum, ciṟu tūṟukaḷ pampiya kāṭṭai aril, aṟal, patukkai eṉṟum mika mutirntu muṟṟippōṉa maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭai mutai eṉṟum, maraṅkaḷ karintu pōṉa kāṭṭaip poccai, curam, poti eṉṟum aracaṉatu kāvaliluḷḷa kāṭṭaik kaṇaiyam, miḷai, araṇ eṉṟum paṇṭutoṭṭut tamiḻmakkaḷ vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam palavakaiyāl vaḻaṅkappaṭum kāṭukaḷil perumpālāṉa malaikaḷiṉ mēlum malaikaḷiṉ kīḻum malaicārnta iṭaṅkaḷilumē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟupāṉmaiya kaṭal cārnta iṭaṅkaḷil irukkiṉṟaṉa. tirumaṟaikkāṭu (vētāraṇiyam) eṉṉum civapirāṉ tirukkōyil uḷḷa ūraiyaṭutta kāṭu kaṭalaṭutta nilattiṉkaṇ uḷḷatu. paṉaṅkāṭu uṭaivēlaṅkāṭu mutaliyaṉa pāṇṭināṭṭil irāmēcuvaram, tiruccentūr mutaliyavaikaṭku aṇmaiyavāṉa kaṭalcārnta iṭaṅkaḷil mikutiyāy uḷḷaṉa.
vāṉ aḷāviya maraṅkaḷ aṭarntu, tolaiviliruntu nōkkuvārkkup pacuntaḻaip pōrvaiyuṭaṉ tōṉṟi, avartam kaṇṇaiyum karuttaiyum kavarvaṉavāy, arukē ceṉṟu tammuḷ nuḻaintu nōkkuvārkkup palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa paruttuyarnta maraṅkaḷum ceṭikoṭikaḷum vāyntaṉavāy, uḷḷē cellaccella iruṇṭa iṭaṅ
kaḷum iṭaiyiṭaiyē pakalavaṉ veḷiccampaṭum cantuveḷikaḷum tam akattu uṭaiyaṉavāy, nīr ūṟṟukaḷum ciṟṟaruvikaḷum āṅkāṅku amaiyappeṟṟu avaṟṟai aṭuttuḷḷa marañceṭi koṭikaḷil palavakaip paṟavaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ olikaḷum nirampap peṟṟavaṉavāy tammuḷ celvāriṉ uḷḷattai accuṟuttum yāṉai puli karaṭi perumpāmpukaḷiṉ aṭiccuvaṭukaḷum uṭaṟpativukaḷum poruntiṉavāy, carukukaḷ pūkkaḷ kaṉikaḷ vittukaḷ iṭaṅkaḷ tōṟum utirap peṟṟaṉavāy, iṭai
yiṭaiyē paṭṭu vīḻntu uḷ iyaṅkuvāriṉ vaḻikaḷai maṟikkum ularnta maraṅkaḷum kōṭukaḷum vaḷārkaḷum muṭkaḷum vērkaḷum koṭikaḷum tutaintaṉavāy uḷḷa peruṅkāṭukaḷaip puṉalūr, nīlamalai, tirukkuṟṟālamalai, aḻakarmalai, pāvanācamalai, veḷḷimalai, marutamalai, cērvarāyaṉmalai, imayamalai mutalāṉa malaip pāṅku
kaḷukku yām ceṉṟa kālaṅkaḷil kaṇṭu, muṉṉaṟiyāta oru perumakiḻcciyum iṟumpūtum eytiṉōm. ittakaiya peruṅkāṭukaḷaip pārātavarkaḷukku, ōraṟivu uyirkaḷiṉ amaippil iṟaivaṉ tōṟṟuvittirukkum vaṉappum uyarnta kāṭciyum viyappum payaṉum oru ciṟitumē viḷaṅkā.
kāṉakattiṉ kaviṉaik kāṉakattilaṉṟi veṟeṅkum kāṇal iyalātu. ettaṉai vaṭivāṉa ilaikaḷ! ettaṉai niṟamāṉa malarkaḷ! ettaṉai cuvaiyāṉa kāykaḷ! ettaṉai iṉippāṉa paḻaṅkaḷ! ettaṉai vakaiyāṉa maraṅkaḷ ceṭikaḷ koṭikaḷ! ammamma! iṟaivaṉ paṭaippiṉ palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa viyattaku amaippum, aḻakum oruṅku potuḷiya aṭavikaḷiṉ viḻumiya taṉmaiyai eṅṅaṉam kūṟuvēm! evvāṟu kāṭṭuvēm! kāṉakaṅkaḷiṉ aṭarntuyarnta tōṟṟattaik kāṇkaiyil uḷḷamum uṇarvum mēluyarntu celkiṉṟaṉa! viyappum putumaiyum karuttaik kavarnteḻukiṉṟaṉa!
avai maṭṭumō! niṉṟa nilaiyil niṟkum marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷākiya ōraṟivuyirkaḷ, iṭam viṭṭup peyarum ēṉai uyirkaḷukku ettaṉai vakaiyiṟ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa! nīrum niḻalum uṇavum tūya kāṟṟum avaikaḷ aṉaittuyirkaṭkum aḷittu varukiṉṟaṉa kāṇmiṉkaḷ! kāṭukaḷ illaiyāyiṉ, veyyavaṉ cuṭukatirkaḷāl vetuppappaṭum innilam nīrppacaiyaṟa vaṟaṇṭu viṭum; āviyāka māṟi vāṉiṉkaṇ ceṉṟa nīr kuḷirkāṟṟu vīcutaṟku vaḻiyiṉmaiyāl tirumpa maḻaiyākak kīḻ iṟaṅkātu. ākavē, uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷait tarum maram ceṭi koṭikaḷum ēṉaip payirkaḷum uṇṭākā. ivvuṇmai, kāṭukaḷai aḻittuviṭum iṭaṅkaḷil maḻai arukip pōtalāl naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
uṇavē aṉṟi, namatuyir vāḻkkaikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta tūya kāṟṟum, kāṭukaḷil tuvaṉṟiya marañceṭi koṭikaḷiṉālēyē nām varappeṟukiṉṟōm. ivōraṟi
vuyirttokutikaḷ mikuntillāmal, ēṉai uyirttokutikaḷē eṅkum mikuntirukkumāyiṉ ivai veḷiviṭum naccukkāṟṟai ivvuyirkaḷē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum uṭ
koṇṭu, ataṉāl nōyppaṭṭuc cila nāṭkaḷil iṟantoḻiyum. maṟṟuk kāṉakaṅkaḷilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa maram ceṭi koṭikaḷ payirkaḷō īraṟivu mutal āṟaṟivu īṟāka uṭaiya ūrvaṉa, nīr vāḻvaṉa, paṟappaṉa, naṭappaṉavākiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷ, paṟavaikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ, makkaḷ mutalāṉa iyaṅkumuyirkaḷ veḷiviṭum naccuk kāṟṟai uḷḷiḻuttu, ataṉ naccut taṉmaiyai nīkkit tūya kāṟṟai veḷiviṭṭu ataṉai eṅkum paravac ceykiṉṟaṉa. ivvuṇmai, payir paccaikaḷ illāta nakaraṅkaḷil iruppavarkaḷ aṭuttaṭuttu nōykaḷāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭu viraivil māyntu pōtalālum, maramceṭi koṭikaḷ niṟainta nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷil iruppavarkaḷ nōyillā yākkaiyiṉarāy nīṇṭakālam naṉku uyir vāḻtalālum iṉitaṟiyappaṭum.
iṉi,  ulakattilē mikapperiya kāṭukaḷ, vaṭa amerikkap peruntēyattiṉ oru pakutiyākiya kaṉaṭā nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇētāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa kāṭukaḷiṉ aḷavu munnūṟṟu eḻupattaintu kōṭi kāṇikaḷākumeṉṟu kaṇakkeṭuttirukkiṟārkaḷ. teṉṉamerikka tēyattilum, ittuṇaip periya kāṭukaḷ iruntālum, avaikaḷil maraṅkaḷ mika aṭarntiruttalālum, koṭiya kāṭṭuvilaṅkukaḷum naccup perumpāmpukaḷum mikuntiruttalālum avaṟṟiṉuḷḷē makkaḷ nuḻaintu ceṉṟu pārttalum avaṟṟai aḷaveṭuttalum iyalavillaiyeṉṟu ārāyntupōyk kāṇum āciriyarkaḷ aṟivikkiṉṟārkaḷ. airōppiyap peruntēyattiṉ vaṭapakutiyil uḷatākiya uruciyā tēyattilum mikapperiya kāṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ aḷavum ciṟitēṟakkuṟaiya munnūṟṟeḻupattu aintu kōṭi kāṇikaḷākumeṉṟum it
tuṟaiyil valla aṟiñar kaṇakkeṭutturaikkiṉṟaṉar.
iṉi uyarattilum parumaṉilum kuṟainta aḷaviṉavāṉa maraṅkaḷ niṟainta kuṟuṅkāṭukaḷ perumpālum ellā nāṭukaḷilum āṅkāṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tirumaṟaik kāṭṭiṟkum kōṭiyakkaraikkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa kāṭṭaik kuṟuṅkāṭu eṉṟē kūṟutal vēṇṭum. irāmēcuvarattiṟkum pāmpaṉukkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa kāṭum kuṟuṅkāṭēyākum. tiruccentūraiyaṭutta neytal nilap pakutikaḷiluḷḷa paṉaṅkāṭu uṭaivēlaṅkāṭukaḷum kuṟuṅkāṭṭil cērntaṉavēyākum.
iṉic, ciṟu tūṟukaḷum kuṟuñceṭikaḷum pampiya kāṭukaḷum uṇṭu. yāmirukkum ippallavapurattiṟku arukil tūṟukaḷum ciṟu ceṭikaḷum niṟainta oru nīṇ
ṭakaṉṟa kāṭu uḷatu. iṉṉōraṉṉa ciṟu kāṭukaḷ, mēṟkūṟiya peruṅkuṟuṅ kāṭukaḷaip pōl kāṭcikku iṉiyaṉavāy illāviṭiṉum, nilattiṉ vaṟaṭciyait taṇittu, ēḻai eḷiyōrkaḷ viṟakuveṭṭi viṟṟup piḻaippataṟkup peritum utaviyākiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, mika mutirntu muṟṟippōṉamaiyāl aṭarntillāmal alaicalāyirukkum ilaikaḷaiyum, avvilaikaḷ tāmum illāmal veṟuṅkōṭukaḷ kompu
kaḷaiyum uṭaiya periya maraṅkaḷ niṟainta kāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷaik kōyamputtūrkkaṭutta veḷḷimalaip peruṅkāṭṭiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē kaṇṭu vantōm. taḻainta paciyamarat tokutikaḷaik kaṇṭukaḷitta kaṇkaḷukku, vaṟṟalāy niṟkum immaraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam varuttattai viḷaivittatu. celvattiṉ ceḻumaiyāl kiḷaiñarum naṇparum cūḻa vāḻntu vantavar oru cilar ciṟitu kālattil accelvattai yiḻantu, cuṟṟattār evarumiṉṟi, uṭampiṉ oḷiyum uḷḷak kiḷarcciyum aṟṟut taṉiniṟṟal pōlavum, iḷamaiyum aḻakum tulaṅkat talaimaiyil vīṟi niṉṟār, piṟar cilar avviḷamaiyum aḻakum talaimaiyum māṟi vaṟṟiya yākkaiyiṉarāy varunti niṟṟal pōlavum, pacceṉṟa potuḷiya iḷantaḻaikaḷ utirntu puṭkaḷum avvilaṅ kiṉaṅkaḷum aṇukap peṟāmal nīrppacaiyaṟṟu vaḷārkaḷākiya paḻutta talaimayirkaḷ kāṟṟil alaiyak kōṭukaḷākiya kaikaḷai nīṭṭi virittut tamatu vāṭṭattaik kuṟippavaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭṭa ammaraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, uyarnilai viraivil māṟappeṟum makkaḷiṉ ivvulakavāḻviṉ nilaimaiyaik kuṟippatoṉṟākavē emakkut tōṉṟiyatu.
iṉik, karinta maraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya poccaikkāṭukaḷ katiravaṉ veppattāl kaṉaṉṟu koṇṭirukkum maṇal veḷikaḷākiya pālai nilaṅkaḷilētām arumaiyāyk kāṇappaṭum.
iṉi, mutaliṟ kūṟiya peruṅkāṭukaḷellām, perumpālum āṅkāṅku aracu celuttum aracarkaḷiṉ kāvalil vaikkappaṭṭu, avarkaṭkup pala vakaiyil ērāḷamāṉa varuvāyait tantu varukiṉṟaṉa. kāṭukaḷil mikutiyāka vaḷarum tēkku, pālai, mutirai, āccā mutalāṉa maraṅkaḷ vīṭukaḷ, toḻiṟcālaikaḷ, kappalkaḷ, paṭakukaḷ mutaliyaṉa amaippa taṟkēṟṟa valivuvāyntu nīṇṭakālam keṭātiruppaṉa vātalāl, avaṟṟāl aracarkaṭku varum varuvāy mikka aḷaviṉatu. mēlpāl uḷḷa cālaṭi nāṭṭil iṟṟaikku aiyāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nākarikattiṟciṟantu viḷaṅkiya aracarkaḷ amaitta periya kōyilkaḷum araṇmaṉaikaḷum, namatu tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mēlkaraimalaikaḷil 
vaḷarā niṉṟa tēkku maraṅkaḷaik koṇṭē kaṭṭappaṭṭa pāṉmaiyiṉaip paḻaiya varalāṟṟu nūlāciriyarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintirukkiṉṟaṉar. immaraṅkaḷēyaṉṟi akil cantaṉam mutalāṉa maṇam kamaḻ maraṅkaḷum, vilaiyuyarnta yāṉaikaḷum, yāṉaik kompukaḷ - elumpukaḷum, māṉtōl - pulittōlkaḷum, mayil tōkaikaḷum, tēṉ kūṭukaḷum, arakkum, nāviyum, palavakaik kāykaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷ vittukkaḷum kāṭṭiṉ kaṇiruntu nirampap peṟutaliṉ, ivaikaḷālum aracarkaḷ mikunta varuvāyai aṭaikiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu.
5. marutam - viḷainilam
neṟpayir viḷaiyāniṉṟa vayalnilattaiyum vayalcūḻnta iṭattaiyum marutam eṉṟu tamiḻnūlār paṇṭaikkālantoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. paḻaiya kālattil neṟ payiriṭutaṟkēṟṟavaikaḷākat tiruttappaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷ, maruta maraṅkaḷiṉ arukēyiruntamaiyāṟpōlum, avaṟṟiṟku maruta nilam eṉa avar peyar amaittaṉar. vayalnilattaip paṇṇai, paṇai, cey, ceṟu, kaḻaṉi, paḻaṉam, viḷaiyuḷ, pulam, varappuḷ eṉṉum pala coṟkaḷāl tamiḻ makkaḷ vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar.
paḻaiya nāṭkaḷil māntariṉ tokai perukap peruka, avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya aḷavu uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷ malai kaḷilum, kaṭalkaḷilum, kāṭukaḷilum kiṭaippatu aritāyiṟṟu. malaikaḷilum, kāṭukaḷilum uḷḷa kaṉikaḷ, vittukkaḷ, kiḻaṅkukaḷaiyum māṉ, marai, kaṭampai, āṭu, māṭu, paṉṟi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷaiyum kaṭalkaḷiluḷḷa mīṉkaḷaiyum tiṉṟu vanta mikap paḻaiya muṉṉōrkaḷ, tamatu tokai perukap peruka avai pōtāmai kaṇṭu, āṭu māṭukaḷai mikutiyāka vaḷarttu avaṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷāṉa ney, pāl, tayir mutaliyavai kaḷaiyum kāṭukaḷil āṅkāṅku iyaṟkaiyāka vaḷarntu kiṭanta avarai, tuvarai, moccai, kārāmaṇi, uḻuntu, payaṟu, kaṭalai, eḷḷu mutalāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum uṭkoṇṭu uyir vāḻntu vantārkaḷ. kālañ cellac cella avarkaḷiṉ tokai piṉṉum piṉṉum perukalāṉamaiyiṉ, iyaṟkaiyil kiṭaitta ittaṉai uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷum avarkaṭkup pōtā āyiṉa. itaṟkeṉ ceyvatu eṉṟu, avarkaḷuḷ aṟivāl mikka cilar eṇṇip pārkkap pārkka, iyaṟkaiyāka vaḷarum kūlaṅkaḷiṉ vittukkaḷai maṭṭum eṭuttu, vēṟu marañceṭikoṭikaḷ illāta veḷi nilaṅkaḷaik kīṟi avaṟṟuḷ vitaittāl, avai mikutiyāṉa viḷaiccalait tarumeṉak kaṇṭaṟintārkaḷ. aṟintapiṉ aṟiñarkaḷum avaratu ēvalvaḻi niṉṟavarkaḷum veṭṭa veḷiyākakkiṭanta nilaṅkaḷaik kōlkaḷālum, kuntālikaḷālum kiḷaṟik kotti avaṟṟiṉkaṇ avvitaikaḷai iṭa, avai vēṇṭiya aḷavu veyilum maḻaiyum peṟṟu, vēṟu marañceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ iṭaiñcal illāmaiyiṉ, oṉṟu nūṟākap payaṉtaralāyiṉa.
